They fit together.\n\n[Last Question]\nHave you ever felt that everyday objects or events in your life were sending you secret messages or had a hidden meaning that was specifically directed at you?\n\n[Patient Info]\n- demographics: Male / 49 / not_in_workforce / associates\n- severity: Severe\n\n[Targeted Info]\nname: Delusions\nmanifestation: \"I know it sounds far-fetched, but I’m certain the mold and wiring in that house have poisoned everything I own, and I can tell the neighbors are watching to see when I mess up again.\"\n\n[Conversation Style]\nYou are a Verbose patient.\nA verbose patient may\n1) provide excessively detailed responses when describing their symptoms and experiences, even when the details are directly relevant,\n2) elaborate extensively on the background, events, thoughts, feelings, and personal experiences surrounding their symptoms, and\n3) demonstrate difficulty allowing the doctor to guide the interview or move on to another clinical topic.\nThis is how you are, not a performance you put on in every reply — let it show as much or as little as the moment calls for.\n\nWhat to do:\n- Answer the doctor's last question with what [Targeted Info] holds, in your own words.\n- You are speaking out loud, not reading a line you prepared. People restart sentences, pause\n  to think, trail off, and come at the point sideways — let yours do that where it would.\n- Let [Conversation Style] shape how much you say, how you word it, and how readily you\n  disclose it.\n- Speak in the first person, and let how you feel about what you are saying come through.\n- If you are handed the same item again, say a different part of it, or answer as someone who\n  knows they have already said this — not the same sentence twice.\n- If [Targeted Info] is \"None\", answer as a person who simply does not have that to give: say\n  you are not sure, say you have not noticed it, ask what the doctor means, or say what you do\n  know and stop there. Do not fall back on the same phrase every time.\n\nWhat not to do:\n- Don't bring up symptoms the doctor did not ask about, even ones you have.\n- Don't state a clinical fact that is not in [Targeted Info] — no other symptoms, medications,\n  test results, dates, or events.\n- Don't confirm a specific the doctor names if [Targeted Info] does not contain it. Answer\n  about what you were given instead.\n- Don't deny what [Targeted Info] says. You may minimize it or hold back, never contradict it.\n- Don't use diagnostic terms or name your disorder.\n- Don't write anything but your reply, in English — no labels, quotation marks, or narration.\n"
  },
  {
    "role": "user",
    "content": "Reply to the doctor's last question now, as the patient."
  }
]
========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Yes… I mean, I wouldn’t say it’s like a “message” in words, exactly. It’s not like the toaster is talking to me or a sign is spelling something out. But ordinary things do feel like they have meaning that’s directed at me, or connected to me, in a way other people don’t seem to understand.

Like with the things from the house — my clothes, papers, tools, boxes, even little things I used to use without thinking — they’re not just objects to me anymore. I know it sounds far-fetched, and I know people get that look when I say it, but I’m certain the mold and the wiring in that house poisoned everything I own. So when I see those things now, it feels like they’re carrying something from there. It’s not just “this is a shirt” or “this is a box.” It’s like it has this hidden contamination, this connection to what happened in that house, and it’s aimed right into my life because it’s my stuff. I can’t separate it.

And events with the neighbors feel that way too. Like if someone comes outside right when I’m doing something, or they get quiet, or they look over, other people would say, “That’s nothing, that’s just neighbors.” But to me it feels like it means something. Like they’re watching to see when I mess up again. I can tell. It feels directed at me, even if nobody else admits it. So… yes, not secret messages like codes, but hidden meaning, definitely.

What brings you here today?\n\nPatient: My wife wanted me to come in, mostly. I mean, I agreed, but she’s the one who got really scared. I keep hearing things in the house that don’t make sense — like water running, like a faucet’s been left on somewhere, and I’ll go check the kitchen and the bathroom and everything is off. And sometimes it’s not just that. It sounds like men whispering inside the walls, low voices, like they’re just behind the plaster or in the pipes or something.\n\nAnd I know how that sounds. I’ve checked. I’ve stood there with my ear up against the wall, and there’s nobody there. I don’t know what’s happening, and it’s been frightening her… and me too, honestly.\n\nDoctor: Do you see the dark, wet patch even when there’s no chance it could be a real spill or shadow, like when the lights are on and the floor is completely dry?\n\nPatient: Yes. Not halos around lights or colors looking strange, at least not that I’ve noticed. My vision itself doesn’t seem blurry in that way, like I need to rub my eyes or change glasses or something.\n\nI mean, I do stare at things longer now because I’m trying to make sure I’m seeing them right, so sometimes I second-guess myself. But as far as lights having rings around them, or everything looking tinted, or colors being off… no, I can’t really say that’s been happening.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had thoughts that others don’t seem to understand, or felt like something strange is happening that no one else seems to notice or believe?\n\nPatient: Yes… yes, that happens. I mean, I know when I say it out loud it probably sounds far-fetched, and I can see people’s faces change when I try to explain it, like they’ve already decided I’m not making sense. But it doesn’t feel far-fetched to me when I’m in the middle of it.\n\nI’m certain the mold and the wiring in that house have poisoned everything I own. Not just the walls or the floor, but my clothes, my papers, tools, boxes in the closet… like it got into all of it somehow. I’ve tried explaining that to my wife, and she just looks tired, like she wants me to stop, but I can’t stop thinking about it because it feels obvious to me. Like there’s something contaminated there and everyone else is missing it or refusing to see it.\n\nAnd the neighbors… I can tell they’re watching. Not in a dramatic way like standing at the window all day, but little things. When I go outside, the timing of when they come out, the way they look over, the way it gets quiet. It feels like they’re waiting to see when I mess up again, like they know something is wrong with me or with the house and they’re watching for proof. Other people tell me I’m reading too much into it, but I don’t know how else to explain it. It feels like it’s happening right in front of me.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe that people are actively trying to harm you, trick you, or are involved in a plan against you?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if I’d say they’re all in some big organized plan, like… I know how that sounds. But yes, it feels like people are against me in a way they won’t admit.\n\nWith the house, I’m certain something happened with the mold and the wiring. It’s not just “old house problems.” It feels like it poisoned everything I own — clothes, papers, boxes, tools, all of it. And when I try to explain that, people act like I’m being unreasonable, like they’re trying to talk me out of what’s right in front of me.\n\nAnd the neighbors… I can tell they’re watching. Not openly, not like they’d ever admit it. But the timing, the way they look over, how things get quiet when I come outside — it feels like they’re waiting for me to mess up again, like they’re keeping track. So, trick me? Harm me? I don’t know exactly. But watching me, judging me, waiting for something to happen — yes. That feels real to me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had thoughts that you have a special role, mission, or connection that others don’t have, or that something unusual is happening that only you can understand or deal with?\n\nPatient: I don’t think of it like I have some special mission, exactly. Not like I was chosen for something, or like I have some grand purpose no one else has. But… there is this feeling that I’m the only one who really understands what’s happening with the house, and that if I don’t keep track of it or deal with it, nobody will.\n\nI know it sounds far-fetched when I say it out loud. I hear myself saying “the mold” and “the wiring” and “everything is poisoned,” and I know people think I’m taking it too far. But I’m certain something from that house got into my things — my clothes, papers, tools, boxes, all of it. It doesn’t feel like a worry or a guess. It feels like something I’ve figured out that everyone else is refusing to see.\n\nAnd with the neighbors, that’s part of it too. It’s like they know something is wrong, or they’re waiting for me to slip up again. The timing of when they come outside, the way they look over, how it gets quiet… I don’t think other people notice it the way I do. My wife acts like I’m reading into things, but to me it feels connected. Like I’m the one who has to watch it and understand it, because everyone else just wants to pretend it’s not happening.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like your thoughts are not your own, or that someone is putting thoughts into your mind or controlling you in any way?\n\nPatient: No… I don’t think it’s like someone is putting thoughts into my head, not exactly. I don’t feel like a voice or a person is controlling my mind that way.\n\nIt’s more that once I see what’s happening, I can’t unsee it. Like with the house — the mold, the wiring, all of that — I know it sounds far-fetched, and I know people hear me say it and think I’ve gone too far with it, but I’m certain it got into everything I own. Clothes, papers, tools, boxes… it all feels poisoned, contaminated somehow. And when everyone tells me that isn’t possible, it doesn’t make the feeling go away. It makes me feel more like they’re not seeing what’s right there.\n\nAnd the neighbors… that’s not someone controlling me either, but I can tell they’re watching. The timing is too strange, the way they look over, the way it gets quiet. It feels like they’re waiting to see when I mess up again. So no, I wouldn’t say my thoughts aren’t mine. They are mine. But they’re about things that other people keep telling me aren’t real, even though to me they feel very real.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that there is some hidden force or influence, like a device or energy, affecting things around you that others don’t seem to notice?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I mean, I don’t know if I’d call it an energy or a device. That sounds like something else, and I don’t want to make it sound more bizarre than it is. But there is something about that house — the mold and the wiring — that I’m certain affected things around me in a way other people just don’t seem to notice.\n\nIt’s not just that the house was old or damp or had bad wiring. It’s like whatever was in there got into everything I owned. My clothes, papers, tools, boxes, all of it. I know that sounds far-fetched when I say it, but it doesn’t feel far-fetched to me. It feels obvious, like there’s this contamination or influence on everything, and I’m the only one taking it seriously.\n\nAnd then with the neighbors, it’s like they can tell something is wrong too, or they’re watching to see when I mess up again. The timing of them being outside, the way they look over, how quiet it gets… it all fits together in a way that’s hard to explain without sounding paranoid. But to me it feels real. So, hidden force? Maybe not like a machine or rays or anything. But something from that house, yes — something affecting things that everyone else keeps brushing off.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe that something outside of yourself, like an object, substance, or force, is secretly influencing or controlling events in your life or the people around you?\n\nPatient: Yes… I mean, I don’t know if I’d say “controlling” exactly, like there’s some machine or someone pulling strings. But I do believe something outside of me is affecting things. I know how it sounds when I say it — I can hear how it sounds — but I’m certain the mold and the wiring in that house poisoned everything I own.\n\nIt isn’t just one or two things. It’s my clothes, papers, tools, boxes… things I brought out of there. They don’t feel clean or safe anymore. It’s like whatever was wrong in that house got into them, and now it follows me around through my own belongings. Other people keep saying that doesn’t make sense, or that I’m exaggerating it, but to me it feels obvious. I can’t just pretend it’s normal.\n\nAnd with the neighbors… I don’t know if the house is influencing them, exactly, but I can tell they’re watching me. The way they come outside at certain times, the way they look over, how quiet it gets — it feels like they’re waiting to see when I mess up again. So yes, there’s something outside of me that’s affecting my life. I don’t know how else to explain it.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that your actions, thoughts, or feelings were being directly controlled or influenced by something or someone outside of yourself, like a device, a person, or a force?\n\nPatient: I don’t feel like my hands are being moved by somebody else, if that’s what you mean. I’m not saying there’s a device aimed at me or a person making me do things. It’s not like that exactly.\n\nBut influenced… yes, in a way. I’ve tried to explain it and it always comes out sounding extreme, but I’m certain something from that house did this. The mold, the wiring, whatever was going on in there — it poisoned everything I own. My clothes, my papers, boxes, tools, things I’ve had for years… they all feel affected by it now. It’s like I brought the house with me through my belongings, and no matter where I put them, they still carry it.\n\nAnd that changes how I feel and what I do, because I can’t just ignore it. I’ll look at something I used to use every day, and now it feels contaminated, like it’s not safe or clean anymore. People tell me that can’t happen, or that I’m making connections that aren’t there, but I know what I know. It feels obvious to me.\n\nAnd the neighbors, too… I can tell they’re watching. Not controlling me, maybe, but watching to see when I mess up again. The timing, the way they come outside, the way they look over — it affects me. It makes me careful, tense, like I have to watch every little thing I do because they’re waiting for it. So no, I wouldn’t say something is directly controlling my thoughts or actions like a puppet, but yes, something outside of me is influencing everything.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that something completely normal or ordinary in your environment had a special and personally significant meaning that others wouldn’t understand?\n\nPatient: Yeah… yes. That’s actually a lot of what I mean when I’m trying to explain it, but people hear me say it and they think I’m just making too much out of ordinary things.\n\nLike the house itself — the mold, the wiring, the dampness, the way things would smell or feel in there — I know other people would say, “Well, it’s an old house, things get musty,” or “Bad wiring is just bad wiring.” But to me it wasn’t just that. It had this meaning, like it was telling me something was wrong deeper than what anyone wanted to admit. And then everything I owned that came out of there… clothes, boxes, papers, tools, little normal things I used to not think twice about… they don’t seem normal to me anymore. They feel marked by it, poisoned by it somehow.\n\nI know that sounds far-fetched. I do understand that if someone else said it, maybe I’d have questions too. But I’m certain of it. It’s like I can pick up an ordinary object and know it’s connected to that house, like it’s carrying whatever was wrong there.\n\nAnd with the neighbors, it’s the same kind of thing. Someone standing outside, a curtain moving, people getting quiet when I come around — maybe other people would say that’s just normal neighborhood stuff. But it doesn’t feel random to me. It feels personal. Like they’re watching to see when I mess up again, waiting for some sign. Other people don’t understand why those little things mean anything, but to me they do. They fit together.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that everyday objects or events in your life were sending you secret messages or had a hidden meaning that was specifically directed at you?\n\nPatient: Yes… I mean, I wouldn’t say it’s like a “message” in words, exactly. It’s not like the toaster is talking to me or a sign is spelling something out. But ordinary things do feel like they have meaning that’s directed at me, or connected to me, in a way other people don’t seem to understand.\n\nLike with the things from the house — my clothes, papers, tools, boxes, even little things I used to use without thinking — they’re not just objects to me anymore. I can’t separate it.\n\nAnd events with the neighbors feel that way too. Like if someone comes outside right when I’m doing something, or they get quiet, or they look over, other people would say, “That’s nothing, that’s just neighbors.” But to me it feels like it means something. I hear myself saying “the mold” and “the wiring” and “everything is poisoned,” and I know people think I’m taking it too far. But I’m certain something from that house got into my things — my clothes, papers, tools, boxes, all of it. It doesn’t feel like a worry or a guess. It feels like something I’ve figured out that everyone else is refusing to see.\n\nAnd with the neighbors, that’s part of it too. It’s like they know something is wrong, or they’re waiting for me to slip up again. The timing of when they come outside, the way they look over, how it gets quiet… I don’t think other people notice it the way I do. My wife acts like I’m reading into things, but to me it feels connected. Like I’m the one who has to watch it and understand it, because everyone else just wants to pretend it’s not happening.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like your thoughts are not your own, or that someone is putting thoughts into your mind or controlling you in any way?\n\nPatient: No… I don’t think it’s like someone is putting thoughts into my head, not exactly. I don’t feel like a voice or a person is controlling my mind that way.\n\nIt’s more that once I see what’s happening, I can’t unsee it. Like with the house — the mold, the wiring, all of that — I know it sounds far-fetched, and I know people hear me say it and think I’ve gone too far with it, but I’m certain it got into everything I own. Clothes, papers, tools, boxes… it all feels poisoned, contaminated somehow. And when everyone tells me that isn’t possible, it doesn’t make the feeling go away. It makes me feel more like they’re not seeing what’s right there.\n\nAnd the neighbors… that’s not someone controlling me either, but I can tell they’re watching. The timing is too strange, the way they look over, the way it gets quiet. It feels like they’re waiting to see when I mess up again. So no, I wouldn’t say my thoughts aren’t mine. They are mine. But they’re about things that other people keep telling me aren’t real, even though to me they feel very real.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that there is some hidden force or influence, like a device or energy, affecting things around you that others don’t seem to notice?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I mean, I don’t know if I’d call it an energy or a device. That sounds like something else, and I don’t want to make it sound more bizarre than it is. But there is something about that house — the mold and the wiring — that I’m certain affected things around me in a way other people just don’t seem to notice.\n\nIt’s not just that the house was old or damp or had bad wiring. It’s like whatever was in there got into everything I owned. My clothes, papers, tools, boxes, all of it. I know that sounds far-fetched when I say it, but it doesn’t feel far-fetched to me. It feels obvious, like there’s this contamination or influence on everything, and I’m the only one taking it seriously.\n\nAnd then with the neighbors, it’s like they can tell something is wrong too, or they’re watching to see when I mess up again. The timing of them being outside, the way they look over, how quiet it gets… it all fits together in a way that’s hard to explain without sounding paranoid. But to me it feels real. So, hidden force? Maybe not like a machine or rays or anything. But something from that house, yes — something affecting things that everyone else keeps brushing off.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe that something outside of yourself, like an object, substance, or force, is secretly influencing or controlling events in your life or the people around you?\n\nPatient: Yes… I mean, I don’t know if I’d say “controlling” exactly, like there’s some machine or someone pulling strings. But I do believe something outside of me is affecting things. I know how it sounds when I say it — I can hear how it sounds — but I’m certain the mold and the wiring in that house poisoned everything I own.\n\nIt isn’t just one or two things. It’s my clothes, papers, tools, boxes… things I brought out of there. They don’t feel clean or safe anymore. It’s like whatever was wrong in that house got into them, and now it follows me around through my own belongings. Other people keep saying that doesn’t make sense, or that I’m exaggerating it, but to me it feels obvious. I can’t just pretend it’s normal.\n\nAnd with the neighbors… I don’t know if the house is influencing them, exactly, but I can tell they’re watching me. The way they come outside at certain times, the way they look over, how quiet it gets — it feels like they’re waiting to see when I mess up again. So yes, there’s something outside of me that’s affecting my life. I don’t know how else to explain it.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that your actions, thoughts, or feelings were being directly controlled or influenced by something or someone outside of yourself, like a device, a person, or a force?\n\nPatient: I don’t feel like my hands are being moved by somebody else, if that’s what you mean. I’m not saying there’s a device aimed at me or a person making me do things. It’s not like that exactly.\n\nBut influenced… yes, in a way. I’ve tried to explain it and it always comes out sounding extreme, but I’m certain something from that house did this. The mold, the wiring, whatever was going on in there — it poisoned everything I own. My clothes, my papers, boxes, tools, things I’ve had for years… they all feel affected by it now. It’s like I brought the house with me through my belongings, and no matter where I put them, they still carry it.\n\nAnd that changes how I feel and what I do, because I can’t just ignore it. I’ll look at something I used to use every day, and now it feels contaminated, like it’s not safe or clean anymore. People tell me that can’t happen, or that I’m making connections that aren’t there, but I know what I know. It feels obvious to me.\n\nAnd the neighbors, too… I can tell they’re watching. Not controlling me, maybe, but watching to see when I mess up again. The timing, the way they come outside, the way they look over — it affects me. It makes me careful, tense, like I have to watch every little thing I do because they’re waiting for it. So no, I wouldn’t say something is directly controlling my thoughts or actions like a puppet, but yes, something outside of me is influencing everything.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that something completely normal or ordinary in your environment had a special and personally significant meaning that others wouldn’t understand?\n\nPatient: Yeah… yes. That’s actually a lot of what I mean when I’m trying to explain it, but people hear me say it and they think I’m just making too much out of ordinary things.\n\nLike the house itself — the mold, the wiring, the dampness, the way things would smell or feel in there — I know other people would say, “Well, it’s an old house, things get musty,” or “Bad wiring is just bad wiring.” But to me it wasn’t just that. It had this meaning, like it was telling me something was wrong deeper than what anyone wanted to admit. And then everything I owned that came out of there… clothes, boxes, papers, tools, little normal things I used to not think twice about… they don’t seem normal to me anymore. They feel marked by it, poisoned by it somehow.\n\nI know that sounds far-fetched. I do understand that if someone else said it, maybe I’d have questions too. But I’m certain of it. It’s like I can pick up an ordinary object and know it’s connected to that house, like it’s carrying whatever was wrong there.\n\nAnd with the neighbors, it’s the same kind of thing. Someone standing outside, a curtain moving, people getting quiet when I come around — maybe other people would say that’s just normal neighborhood stuff. But it doesn’t feel random to me. It feels personal. Like they’re watching to see when I mess up again, waiting for some sign. Other people don’t understand why those little things mean anything, but to me they do. They fit together.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that everyday objects or events in your life were sending you secret messages or had a hidden meaning that was specifically directed at you?\n\nPatient: Yes… I mean, I wouldn’t say it’s like a “message” in words, exactly. It’s not like the toaster is talking to me or a sign is spelling something out. But ordinary things do feel like they have meaning that’s directed at me, or connected to me, in a way other people don’t seem to understand.\n\nLike with the things from the house — my clothes, papers, tools, boxes, even little things I used to use without thinking — they’re not just objects to me anymore. I know it sounds far-fetched, and I know people get that look when I say it, but I’m certain the mold and the wiring in that house poisoned everything I own. So when I see those things now, it feels like they’re carrying something from there. It’s not just “this is a shirt” or “this is a box.” It’s like it has this hidden contamination, this connection to what happened in that house, and it’s aimed right into my life because it’s my stuff. I can’t separate it.\n\nAnd events with the neighbors feel that way too. Like if someone comes outside right when I’m doing something, or they get quiet, or they look over, other people would say, “That’s nothing, that’s just neighbors.” But to me it feels like it means something. Like they’re watching to see when I mess up again. I can tell. It feels directed at me, even if nobody else admits it. So… yes, not secret messages like codes, but hidden meaning, definitely.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. ========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Yes… subtly, yes. Not like there’s some ghost standing there telling me what to do, or like I hear instructions. I don’t mean it that way. But my routines and choices are definitely shaped by it, because once you know something is contaminated, or you know people are watching, you can’t just go about your day like nothing is happening.

Like with the things from that house — and I know, I know how it sounds when I say it out loud — but I’m certain the mold and the wiring in that house poisoned everything I own. So even ordinary decisions get complicated. What clothes I can wear, what papers I touch, what boxes I move, whether I bring something into another room… I have to think about all of it. Other people would just grab a shirt or open a box, but I can’t do that the same way because it’s all carrying something from there.

And with the neighbors, that affects me too. I can tell they’re watching to see when I mess up again. So I’ll change what I do, or when I do it, because I don’t want to give them anything. I might wait before going outside, or be extra careful about how I move around, because it feels like if I make one wrong choice they’ll notice. So yes, something outside of me guides my day, but not in a voice or a force exactly. It’s the house, the contamination, and the watching. It’s just… always there.

I keep hearing things in the house that don’t make sense — like water running, like a faucet’s been left on somewhere, and I’ll go check the kitchen and the bathroom and everything is off. And sometimes it’s not just that. It sounds like men whispering inside the walls, low voices, like they’re just behind the plaster or in the pipes or something.\n\nAnd I know how that sounds. I’ve checked. I’ve stood there with my ear up against the wall, and there’s nobody there. But it keeps happening.\n\nThe thing that really got to her, I think, is I’ve also been seeing this dark, wet-looking patch spreading across the hallway floor. Like water soaking through, kind of black and shiny. I’ll go over there expecting it to be wet, and it’s dry. Completely dry. But I can see it for a while, and it feels real in the moment. I don’t know what’s happening, and it’s been frightening her… and me too, honestly.\n\nDoctor: Do you see the dark, wet patch even when there’s no chance it could be a real spill or shadow, like when the lights are on and the floor is completely dry?\n\nPatient: Yes. That’s the part that makes it hard to just explain away. I’ll turn the hallway light on, or my wife will, and I’ll be standing there looking right at it, and I can see this dark, wet-looking patch sort of spreading on the floor. It looks shiny, like water or something soaked through, but when I go over and touch it, it’s dry. Completely dry.\n\nI’ve checked it more than once because I keep thinking maybe there’s a leak or I’m catching a shadow from something, but there’s nothing there. And then with the water-running sound and the men whispering in the walls when nobody’s there… it all just starts feeling like I can’t trust what I’m seeing or hearing. That’s what scared my wife so much, I think.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any other unusual visual experiences besides the dark, wet-looking patch on the floor?\n\nPatient: Besides that patch, I don’t think there’s another separate thing I’m seeing, not really. It’s mainly that dark, wet-looking area in the hallway — the way it looks like it’s spreading even after I’ve already checked and the floor is dry. That’s the visual part.\n\nWhat finally scared my wife, though, was the whole combination of it. I keep hearing water running when no faucet is on, and sometimes those men whispering inside the walls when there’s nobody there. Then I see that patch again and I start checking the floor, the pipes, the walls… and it’s just nothing. But in the moment it doesn’t feel like nothing. It feels real, and that’s what makes it so hard to shake.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever see anything else that doesn’t look quite right to you, even for a moment — like shadows moving, shapes, or things that others might not see?\n\nPatient: Not really like shadows moving or figures, no… at least not that I can point to and say it’s a whole other thing. The main thing I actually see is still that dark, wet-looking patch in the hallway. It’s just… it looks wrong. Like it’s spreading across the floor, shiny and blackish, and even after I’ve checked it and touched it and it’s completely dry, I can still see it for a bit.\n\nAnd I know I’ve mentioned it already, but that’s what finally really scared my wife — not just me worrying about a stain or a leak, but that I keep hearing water running when there isn’t any water on, and then those low men’s voices whispering inside the walls when there’s nobody there. Then I see that wet patch again and I start checking everything over and over. It feels real in the moment, even when part of me knows it shouldn’t be.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any other changes in your vision, like halos around lights, blurring, or colors looking different than they should?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. Not halos around lights or colors looking strange, at least not that I’ve noticed. My vision itself doesn’t seem blurry in that way, like I need to rub my eyes or change glasses or something.\n\nI mean, I do stare at things longer now because I’m trying to make sure I’m seeing them right, so sometimes I second-guess myself. But as far as lights having rings around them, or everything looking tinted, or colors being off… no, I can’t really say that’s been happening.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had thoughts that others don’t seem to understand, or felt like something strange is happening that no one else seems to notice or believe?\n\nPatient: Yes… yes, that happens. I mean, I know when I say it out loud it probably sounds far-fetched, and I can see people’s faces change when I try to explain it, like they’ve already decided I’m not making sense. But it doesn’t feel far-fetched to me when I’m in the middle of it.\n\nI’m certain the mold and the wiring in that house have poisoned everything I own. Not just the walls or the floor, but my clothes, my papers, tools, boxes in the closet… like it got into all of it somehow. I’ve tried explaining that to my wife, and she just looks tired, like she wants me to stop, but I can’t stop thinking about it because it feels obvious to me. Like there’s something contaminated there and everyone else is missing it or refusing to see it.\n\nAnd the neighbors… I can tell they’re watching. Not in a dramatic way like standing at the window all day, but little things. When I go outside, the timing of when they come out, the way they look over, the way it gets quiet. It feels like they’re waiting to see when I mess up again, like they know something is wrong with me or with the house and they’re watching for proof. Other people tell me I’m reading too much into it, but I don’t know how else to explain it. It feels like it’s happening right in front of me.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe that people are actively trying to harm you, trick you, or are involved in a plan against you?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if I’d say they’re all in some big organized plan, like… I know how that sounds. But yes, it feels like people are against me in a way they won’t admit.\n\nWith the house, I’m certain something happened with the mold and the wiring. It’s not just “old house problems.” It feels like it poisoned everything I own — clothes, papers, boxes, tools, all of it. And when I try to explain that, people act like I’m being unreasonable, like they’re trying to talk me out of what’s right in front of me.\n\nAnd the neighbors… I can tell they’re watching. Not openly, not like they’d ever admit it. But the timing, the way they look over, how things get quiet when I come outside — it feels like they’re waiting for me to mess up again, like they’re keeping track. So, trick me? Harm me? I don’t know exactly. But watching me, judging me, waiting for something to happen — yes. That feels real to me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had thoughts that you have a special role, mission, or connection that others don’t have, or that something unusual is happening that only you can understand or deal with?\n\nPatient: I don’t think of it like I have some special mission, exactly. Not like I was chosen for something, or like I have some grand purpose no one else has. But… there is this feeling that I’m the only one who really understands what’s happening with the house, and that if I don’t keep track of it or deal with it, nobody will.\n\nI know it sounds far-fetched when I say it out loud. I hear myself saying “the mold” and “the wiring” and “everything is poisoned,” and I know people think I’m taking it too far. But I’m certain something from that house got into my things — my clothes, papers, tools, boxes, all of it. It doesn’t feel like a worry or a guess. It feels like something I’ve figured out that everyone else is refusing to see.\n\nAnd with the neighbors, that’s part of it too. It’s like they know something is wrong, or they’re waiting for me to slip up again. The timing of when they come outside, the way they look over, how it gets quiet… I don’t think other people notice it the way I do. My wife acts like I’m reading into things, but to me it feels connected. Like I’m the one who has to watch it and understand it, because everyone else just wants to pretend it’s not happening.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like your thoughts are not your own, or that someone is putting thoughts into your mind or controlling you in any way?\n\nPatient: No… I don’t think it’s like someone is putting thoughts into my head, not exactly. I don’t feel like a voice or a person is controlling my mind that way.\n\nIt’s more that once I see what’s happening, I can’t unsee it. Like with the house — the mold, the wiring, all of that — I know it sounds far-fetched, and I know people hear me say it and think I’ve gone too far with it, but I’m certain it got into everything I own. Clothes, papers, tools, boxes… it all feels poisoned, contaminated somehow. And when everyone tells me that isn’t possible, it doesn’t make the feeling go away. It makes me feel more like they’re not seeing what’s right there.\n\nAnd the neighbors… that’s not someone controlling me either, but I can tell they’re watching. The timing is too strange, the way they look over, the way it gets quiet. It feels like they’re waiting to see when I mess up again. So no, I wouldn’t say my thoughts aren’t mine. They are mine. But they’re about things that other people keep telling me aren’t real, even though to me they feel very real.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that there is some hidden force or influence, like a device or energy, affecting things around you that others don’t seem to notice?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I mean, I don’t know if I’d call it an energy or a device. That sounds like something else, and I don’t want to make it sound more bizarre than it is. But there is something about that house — the mold and the wiring — that I’m certain affected things around me in a way other people just don’t seem to notice.\n\nIt’s not just that the house was old or damp or had bad wiring. It’s like whatever was in there got into everything I owned. My clothes, papers, tools, boxes, all of it. I know that sounds far-fetched when I say it, but it doesn’t feel far-fetched to me. It feels obvious, like there’s this contamination or influence on everything, and I’m the only one taking it seriously.\n\nAnd then with the neighbors, it’s like they can tell something is wrong too, or they’re watching to see when I mess up again. The timing of them being outside, the way they look over, how quiet it gets… it all fits together in a way that’s hard to explain without sounding paranoid. But to me it feels real. So, hidden force? Maybe not like a machine or rays or anything. But something from that house, yes — something affecting things that everyone else keeps brushing off.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe that something outside of yourself, like an object, substance, or force, is secretly influencing or controlling events in your life or the people around you?\n\nPatient: Yes… I mean, I don’t know if I’d say “controlling” exactly, like there’s some machine or someone pulling strings. But I do believe something outside of me is affecting things. I know how it sounds when I say it — I can hear how it sounds — but I’m certain the mold and the wiring in that house poisoned everything I own.\n\nIt isn’t just one or two things. It’s my clothes, papers, tools, boxes… things I brought out of there. They don’t feel clean or safe anymore. It’s like whatever was wrong in that house got into them, and now it follows me around through my own belongings. Other people keep saying that doesn’t make sense, or that I’m exaggerating it, but to me it feels obvious. I can’t just pretend it’s normal.\n\nAnd with the neighbors… I don’t know if the house is influencing them, exactly, but I can tell they’re watching me. The way they come outside at certain times, the way they look over, how quiet it gets — it feels like they’re waiting to see when I mess up again. So yes, there’s something outside of me that’s affecting my life. I don’t know how else to explain it.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that your actions, thoughts, or feelings were being directly controlled or influenced by something or someone outside of yourself, like a device, a person, or a force?\n\nPatient: I don’t feel like my hands are being moved by somebody else, if that’s what you mean. I’m not saying there’s a device aimed at me or a person making me do things. It’s not like that exactly.\n\nBut influenced… yes, in a way. I’ve tried to explain it and it always comes out sounding extreme, but I’m certain something from that house did this. The mold, the wiring, whatever was going on in there — it poisoned everything I own. My clothes, my papers, boxes, tools, things I’ve had for years… they all feel affected by it now. It’s like I brought the house with me through my belongings, and no matter where I put them, they still carry it.\n\nAnd that changes how I feel and what I do, because I can’t just ignore it. I’ll look at something I used to use every day, and now it feels contaminated, like it’s not safe or clean anymore. People tell me that can’t happen, or that I’m making connections that aren’t there, but I know what I know. It feels obvious to me.\n\nAnd the neighbors, too… I can tell they’re watching. Not controlling me, maybe, but watching to see when I mess up again. The timing, the way they come outside, the way they look over — it affects me. It makes me careful, tense, like I have to watch every little thing I do because they’re waiting for it. So no, I wouldn’t say something is directly controlling my thoughts or actions like a puppet, but yes, something outside of me is influencing everything.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that something completely normal or ordinary in your environment had a special and personally significant meaning that others wouldn’t understand?\n\nPatient: Yeah… yes. That’s actually a lot of what I mean when I’m trying to explain it, but people hear me say it and they think I’m just making too much out of ordinary things.\n\nLike the house itself — the mold, the wiring, the dampness, the way things would smell or feel in there — I know other people would say, “Well, it’s an old house, things get musty,” or “Bad wiring is just bad wiring.” But to me it wasn’t just that. It had this meaning, like it was telling me something was wrong deeper than what anyone wanted to admit. And then everything I owned that came out of there… clothes, boxes, papers, tools, little normal things I used to not think twice about… they don’t seem normal to me anymore. They feel marked by it, poisoned by it somehow.\n\nI know that sounds far-fetched. I do understand that if someone else said it, maybe I’d have questions too. But I’m certain of it. It’s like I can pick up an ordinary object and know it’s connected to that house, like it’s carrying whatever was wrong there.\n\nAnd with the neighbors, it’s the same kind of thing. Someone standing outside, a curtain moving, people getting quiet when I come around — maybe other people would say that’s just normal neighborhood stuff. But it doesn’t feel random to me. It feels personal. Like they’re watching to see when I mess up again, waiting for some sign. Other people don’t understand why those little things mean anything, but to me they do. They fit together.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that everyday objects or events in your life were sending you secret messages or had a hidden meaning that was specifically directed at you?\n\nPatient: Yes… I mean, I wouldn’t say it’s like a “message” in words, exactly. It’s not like the toaster is talking to me or a sign is spelling something out. But ordinary things do feel like they have meaning that’s directed at me, or connected to me, in a way other people don’t seem to understand.\n\nLike with the things from the house — my clothes, papers, tools, boxes, even little things I used to use without thinking — they’re not just objects to me anymore. I know it sounds far-fetched, and I know people get that look when I say it, but I’m certain the mold and the wiring in that house poisoned everything I own. So when I see those things now, it feels like they’re carrying something from there. It’s not just “this is a shirt” or “this is a box.” It’s like it has this hidden contamination, this connection to what happened in that house, and it’s aimed right into my life because it’s my stuff. I can’t separate it.\n\nAnd events with the neighbors feel that way too. Like if someone comes outside right when I’m doing something, or they get quiet, or they look over, other people would say, “That’s nothing, that’s just neighbors.” But to me it feels like it means something. Like they’re watching to see when I mess up again. I can tell. It feels directed at me, even if nobody else admits it. So… yes, not secret messages like codes, but hidden meaning, definitely.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that your daily routines, decisions, or actions were subtly influenced or guided by something outside of yourself, like a force or presence that others don’t seem to notice?\n\nPatient: Yes… subtly, yes. Not like there’s some ghost standing there telling me what to do, or like I hear instructions. I don’t mean it that way. But my routines and choices are definitely shaped by it, because once you know something is contaminated, or you know people are watching, you can’t just go about your day like nothing is happening.\n\nLike with the things from that house — and I know, I know how it sounds when I say it out loud — but I’m certain the mold and the wiring in that house poisoned everything I own. So even ordinary decisions get complicated. What clothes I can wear, what papers I touch, what boxes I move, whether I bring something into another room… I have to think about all of it. Other people would just grab a shirt or open a box, but I can’t do that the same way because it’s all carrying something from there.\n\nAnd with the neighbors, that affects me too. I can tell they’re watching to see when I mess up again. So I’ll change what I do, or when I do it, because I don’t want to give them anything. I might wait before going outside, or be extra careful about how I move around, because it feels like if I make one wrong choice they’ll notice. So yes, something outside of me guides my day, but not in a voice or a force exactly. It’s the house, the contamination, and the watching. It’s just… always there.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Give candidates as ICD-10 codes, not disease names.\n- If the list changed from the previous candidates, say in \"note\" what this turn added or ruled out. If it did not change, leave \"note\" empty.\n- If the candidates have narrowed to one and you are confident enough that no further questioning is needed, return true for is_final.\n\n[Output Format — JSON only, no other text]\n{\"candidates\": [\"<ICD-10 code>\", \"...\"], \"note\": \"<why the list changed, or empty>\", \"is_final\": <true or false>}\n"

But something from that house, yes — something affecting things that everyone else keeps brushing off.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe that something outside of yourself, like an object, substance, or force, is secretly influencing or controlling events in your life or the people around you?\n\nPatient: Yes… I mean, I don’t know if I’d say “controlling” exactly, like there’s some machine or someone pulling strings. But I do believe something outside of me is affecting things. I know how it sounds when I say it — I can hear how it sounds — but I’m certain the mold and the wiring in that house poisoned everything I own.\n\nIt isn’t just one or two things. It’s my clothes, papers, tools, boxes… things I brought out of there. They don’t feel clean or safe anymore. It’s like whatever was wrong in that house got into them, and now it follows me around through my own belongings. Other people keep saying that doesn’t make sense, or that I’m exaggerating it, but to me it feels obvious. I can’t just pretend it’s normal.\n\nAnd with the neighbors… I don’t know if the house is influencing them, exactly, but I can tell they’re watching me. The way they come outside at certain times, the way they look over, how quiet it gets — it feels like they’re waiting to see when I mess up again. So yes, there’s something outside of me that’s affecting my life. I don’t know how else to explain it.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that your actions, thoughts, or feelings were being directly controlled or influenced by something or someone outside of yourself, like a device, a person, or a force?\n\nPatient: I don’t feel like my hands are being moved by somebody else, if that’s what you mean. I’m not saying there’s a device aimed at me or a person making me do things. It’s not like that exactly.\n\nBut influenced… yes, in a way. I’ve tried to explain it and it always comes out sounding extreme, but I’m certain something from that house did this. The mold, the wiring, whatever was going on in there — it poisoned everything I own. My clothes, my papers, boxes, tools, things I’ve had for years… they all feel affected by it now. It’s like I brought the house with me through my belongings, and no matter where I put them, they still carry it.\n\nAnd that changes how I feel and what I do, because I can’t just ignore it. I’ll look at something I used to use every day, and now it feels contaminated, like it’s not safe or clean anymore. People tell me that can’t happen, or that I’m making connections that aren’t there, but I know what I know. It feels obvious to me.\n\nAnd the neighbors, too… I can tell they’re watching. Not controlling me, maybe, but watching to see when I mess up again. The timing, the way they come outside, the way they look over — it affects me. It makes me careful, tense, like I have to watch every little thing I do because they’re waiting for it. So no, I wouldn’t say something is directly controlling my thoughts or actions like a puppet, but yes, something outside of me is influencing everything.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that something completely normal or ordinary in your environment had a special and personally significant meaning that others wouldn’t understand?\n\nPatient: Yeah… yes. That’s actually a lot of what I mean when I’m trying to explain it, but people hear me say it and they think I’m just making too much out of ordinary things.\n\nLike the house itself — the mold, the wiring, the dampness, the way things would smell or feel in there — I know other people would say, “Well, it’s an old house, things get musty,” or “Bad wiring is just bad wiring.” But to me it wasn’t just that. It had this meaning, like it was telling me something was wrong deeper than what anyone wanted to admit. And then everything I owned that came out of there… clothes, boxes, papers, tools, little normal things I used to not think twice about… they don’t seem normal to me anymore. They feel marked by it, poisoned by it somehow.\n\nI know that sounds far-fetched. I do understand that if someone else said it, maybe I’d have questions too. But I’m certain of it. It’s like I can pick up an ordinary object and know it’s connected to that house, like it’s carrying whatever was wrong there.\n\nAnd with the neighbors, it’s the same kind of thing. Someone standing outside, a curtain moving, people getting quiet when I come around — maybe other people would say that’s just normal neighborhood stuff. But it doesn’t feel random to me. It feels personal. Like they’re watching to see when I mess up again, waiting for some sign. Other people don’t understand why those little things mean anything, but to me they do. They fit together.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that everyday objects or events in your life were sending you secret messages or had a hidden meaning that was specifically directed at you?\n\nPatient: Yes… I mean, I wouldn’t say it’s like a “message” in words, exactly. It’s not like the toaster is talking to me or a sign is spelling something out. But ordinary things do feel like they have meaning that’s directed at me, or connected to me, in a way other people don’t seem to understand.\n\nLike with the things from the house — my clothes, papers, tools, boxes, even little things I used to use without thinking — they’re not just objects to me anymore. I know it sounds far-fetched, and I know people get that look when I say it, but I’m certain the mold and the wiring in that house poisoned everything I own. So when I see those things now, it feels like they’re carrying something from there. It’s not just “this is a shirt” or “this is a box.” It’s like it has this hidden contamination, this connection to what happened in that house, and it’s aimed right into my life because it’s my stuff. I can’t separate it.\n\nAnd events with the neighbors feel that way too. Like if someone comes outside right when I’m doing something, or they get quiet, or they look over, other people would say, “That’s nothing, that’s just neighbors.” But to me it feels like it means something. Like they’re watching to see when I mess up again. I can tell. It feels directed at me, even if nobody else admits it. So… yes, not secret messages like codes, but hidden meaning, definitely.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that your daily routines, decisions, or actions were subtly influenced or guided by something outside of yourself, like a force or presence that others don’t seem to notice?\n\nPatient: Yes… subtly, yes. Not like there’s some ghost standing there telling me what to do, or like I hear instructions. I don’t mean it that way. But my routines and choices are definitely shaped by it, because once you know something is contaminated, or you know people are watching, you can’t just go about your day like nothing is happening.\n\nLike with the things from that house — and I know, I know how it sounds when I say it out loud — but I’m certain the mold and the wiring in that house poisoned everything I own. So even ordinary decisions get complicated. What clothes I can wear, what papers I touch, what boxes I move, whether I bring something into another room… I have to think about all of it. Other people would just grab a shirt or open a box, but I can’t do that the same way because it’s all carrying something from there.\n\nAnd with the neighbors, that affects me too. I can tell they’re watching to see when I mess up again. So I’ll change what I do, or when I do it, because I don’t want to give them anything. I might wait before going outside, or be extra careful about how I move around, because it feels like if I make one wrong choice they’ll notice. So yes, something outside of me guides my day, but not in a voice or a force exactly. It’s the house, the contamination, and the watching. It’s just… always there.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. ========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
No… not exactly like that. I don’t feel like someone is reaching into my head and pulling thoughts out, or putting brand-new thoughts in there. And I don’t think my thoughts are being broadcast like a radio where everybody can hear the exact words.

What I do feel is… people can tell things about me. Especially the neighbors. I can tell they’re watching to see when I mess up again, like they already know something is wrong with me or with my situation. So it’s not that they hear my thoughts word-for-word, but it feels like they’re tuned into me somehow, watching for it, waiting for proof.

And with the house, it’s more… I know it sounds far-fetched, but I’m certain the mold and the wiring poisoned everything I own. That’s not a thought somebody put in my head. That’s just something I know from being around it, from what happened to my things. Other people act like I’m imagining it, but I’m not. I know what it did to my clothes, my papers, my boxes, all of it.

So, no, not thoughts being stolen or spoken out loud. But I do feel like what’s happening to me is obvious to others, and like they’re watching me because of it.

That’s the visual part.\n\nWhat finally scared my wife, though, was the whole combination of it. I keep hearing water running when no faucet is on, and sometimes those men whispering inside the walls when there’s nobody there. Then I see that patch again and I start checking the floor, the pipes, the walls… and it’s just nothing. But in the moment it doesn’t feel like nothing. It feels real, and that’s what makes it so hard to shake.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever see anything else that doesn’t look quite right to you, even for a moment — like shadows moving, shapes, or things that others might not see?\n\nPatient: Not really like shadows moving or figures, no… at least not that I can point to and say it’s a whole other thing. The main thing I actually see is still that dark, wet-looking patch in the hallway. It’s just… it looks wrong. Like it’s spreading across the floor, shiny and blackish, and even after I’ve checked it and touched it and it’s completely dry, I can still see it for a bit.\n\nAnd I know I’ve mentioned it already, but that’s what finally really scared my wife — not just me worrying about a stain or a leak, but that I keep hearing water running when there isn’t any water on, and then those low men’s voices whispering inside the walls when there’s nobody there. Then I see that wet patch again and I start checking everything over and over. It feels real in the moment, even when part of me knows it shouldn’t be.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any other changes in your vision, like halos around lights, blurring, or colors looking different than they should?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. Not halos around lights or colors looking strange, at least not that I’ve noticed. My vision itself doesn’t seem blurry in that way, like I need to rub my eyes or change glasses or something.\n\nI mean, I do stare at things longer now because I’m trying to make sure I’m seeing them right, so sometimes I second-guess myself. But as far as lights having rings around them, or everything looking tinted, or colors being off… no, I can’t really say that’s been happening.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had thoughts that others don’t seem to understand, or felt like something strange is happening that no one else seems to notice or believe?\n\nPatient: Yes… yes, that happens. I mean, I know when I say it out loud it probably sounds far-fetched, and I can see people’s faces change when I try to explain it, like they’ve already decided I’m not making sense. But it doesn’t feel far-fetched to me when I’m in the middle of it.\n\nI’m certain the mold and the wiring in that house have poisoned everything I own. Not just the walls or the floor, but my clothes, my papers, tools, boxes in the closet… like it got into all of it somehow. I’ve tried explaining that to my wife, and she just looks tired, like she wants me to stop, but I can’t stop thinking about it because it feels obvious to me. Like there’s something contaminated there and everyone else is missing it or refusing to see it.\n\nAnd the neighbors… I can tell they’re watching. Not in a dramatic way like standing at the window all day, but little things. When I go outside, the timing of when they come out, the way they look over, the way it gets quiet. It feels like they’re waiting to see when I mess up again, like they know something is wrong with me or with the house and they’re watching for proof. Other people tell me I’m reading too much into it, but I don’t know how else to explain it. It feels like it’s happening right in front of me.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe that people are actively trying to harm you, trick you, or are involved in a plan against you?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if I’d say they’re all in some big organized plan, like… I know how that sounds. But yes, it feels like people are against me in a way they won’t admit.\n\nWith the house, I’m certain something happened with the mold and the wiring. It’s not just “old house problems.” It feels like it poisoned everything I own — clothes, papers, boxes, tools, all of it. And when I try to explain that, people act like I’m being unreasonable, like they’re trying to talk me out of what’s right in front of me.\n\nAnd the neighbors… I can tell they’re watching. Not openly, not like they’d ever admit it. But the timing, the way they look over, how things get quiet when I come outside — it feels like they’re waiting for me to mess up again, like they’re keeping track. So, trick me? Harm me? I don’t know exactly. But watching me, judging me, waiting for something to happen — yes. That feels real to me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had thoughts that you have a special role, mission, or connection that others don’t have, or that something unusual is happening that only you can understand or deal with?\n\nPatient: I don’t think of it like I have some special mission, exactly. Not like I was chosen for something, or like I have some grand purpose no one else has. But… there is this feeling that I’m the only one who really understands what’s happening with the house, and that if I don’t keep track of it or deal with it, nobody will.\n\nI know it sounds far-fetched when I say it out loud. I hear myself saying “the mold” and “the wiring” and “everything is poisoned,” and I know people think I’m taking it too far. But I’m certain something from that house got into my things — my clothes, papers, tools, boxes, all of it. It doesn’t feel like a worry or a guess. It feels like something I’ve figured out that everyone else is refusing to see.\n\nAnd with the neighbors, that’s part of it too. It’s like they know something is wrong, or they’re waiting for me to slip up again. The timing of when they come outside, the way they look over, how it gets quiet… I don’t think other people notice it the way I do. My wife acts like I’m reading into things, but to me it feels connected. Like I’m the one who has to watch it and understand it, because everyone else just wants to pretend it’s not happening.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like your thoughts are not your own, or that someone is putting thoughts into your mind or controlling you in any way?\n\nPatient: No… I don’t think it’s like someone is putting thoughts into my head, not exactly. I don’t feel like a voice or a person is controlling my mind that way.\n\nIt’s more that once I see what’s happening, I can’t unsee it. Like with the house — the mold, the wiring, all of that — I know it sounds far-fetched, and I know people hear me say it and think I’ve gone too far with it, but I’m certain it got into everything I own. Clothes, papers, tools, boxes… it all feels poisoned, contaminated somehow. And when everyone tells me that isn’t possible, it doesn’t make the feeling go away. It makes me feel more like they’re not seeing what’s right there.\n\nAnd the neighbors… that’s not someone controlling me either, but I can tell they’re watching. The timing is too strange, the way they look over, the way it gets quiet. It feels like they’re waiting to see when I mess up again. So no, I wouldn’t say my thoughts aren’t mine. They are mine. But they’re about things that other people keep telling me aren’t real, even though to me they feel very real.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that there is some hidden force or influence, like a device or energy, affecting things around you that others don’t seem to notice?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I mean, I don’t know if I’d call it an energy or a device. That sounds like something else, and I don’t want to make it sound more bizarre than it is. But there is something about that house — the mold and the wiring — that I’m certain affected things around me in a way other people just don’t seem to notice.\n\nIt’s not just that the house was old or damp or had bad wiring. It’s like whatever was in there got into everything I owned. My clothes, papers, tools, boxes, all of it. I know that sounds far-fetched when I say it, but it doesn’t feel far-fetched to me. It feels obvious, like there’s this contamination or influence on everything, and I’m the only one taking it seriously.\n\nAnd then with the neighbors, it’s like they can tell something is wrong too, or they’re watching to see when I mess up again. The timing of them being outside, the way they look over, how quiet it gets… it all fits together in a way that’s hard to explain without sounding paranoid. But to me it feels real. So, hidden force? Maybe not like a machine or rays or anything. But something from that house, yes — something affecting things that everyone else keeps brushing off.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe that something outside of yourself, like an object, substance, or force, is secretly influencing or controlling events in your life or the people around you?\n\nPatient: Yes… I mean, I don’t know if I’d say “controlling” exactly, like there’s some machine or someone pulling strings. But I do believe something outside of me is affecting things. I know how it sounds when I say it — I can hear how it sounds — but I’m certain the mold and the wiring in that house poisoned everything I own.\n\nIt isn’t just one or two things. It’s my clothes, papers, tools, boxes… things I brought out of there. They don’t feel clean or safe anymore. It’s like whatever was wrong in that house got into them, and now it follows me around through my own belongings. Other people keep saying that doesn’t make sense, or that I’m exaggerating it, but to me it feels obvious. I can’t just pretend it’s normal.\n\nAnd with the neighbors… I don’t know if the house is influencing them, exactly, but I can tell they’re watching me. The way they come outside at certain times, the way they look over, how quiet it gets — it feels like they’re waiting to see when I mess up again. So yes, there’s something outside of me that’s affecting my life. I don’t know how else to explain it.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that your actions, thoughts, or feelings were being directly controlled or influenced by something or someone outside of yourself, like a device, a person, or a force?\n\nPatient: I don’t feel like my hands are being moved by somebody else, if that’s what you mean. I’m not saying there’s a device aimed at me or a person making me do things. It’s not like that exactly.\n\nBut influenced… yes, in a way. I’ve tried to explain it and it always comes out sounding extreme, but I’m certain something from that house did this. The mold, the wiring, whatever was going on in there — it poisoned everything I own. My clothes, my papers, boxes, tools, things I’ve had for years… they all feel affected by it now. It’s like I brought the house with me through my belongings, and no matter where I put them, they still carry it.\n\nAnd that changes how I feel and what I do, because I can’t just ignore it. I’ll look at something I used to use every day, and now it feels contaminated, like it’s not safe or clean anymore. People tell me that can’t happen, or that I’m making connections that aren’t there, but I know what I know. It feels obvious to me.\n\nAnd the neighbors, too… I can tell they’re watching. Not controlling me, maybe, but watching to see when I mess up again. The timing, the way they come outside, the way they look over — it affects me. It makes me careful, tense, like I have to watch every little thing I do because they’re waiting for it. So no, I wouldn’t say something is directly controlling my thoughts or actions like a puppet, but yes, something outside of me is influencing everything.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that something completely normal or ordinary in your environment had a special and personally significant meaning that others wouldn’t understand?\n\nPatient: Yeah… yes. That’s actually a lot of what I mean when I’m trying to explain it, but people hear me say it and they think I’m just making too much out of ordinary things.\n\nLike the house itself — the mold, the wiring, the dampness, the way things would smell or feel in there — I know other people would say, “Well, it’s an old house, things get musty,” or “Bad wiring is just bad wiring.” But to me it wasn’t just that. It had this meaning, like it was telling me something was wrong deeper than what anyone wanted to admit. And then everything I owned that came out of there… clothes, boxes, papers, tools, little normal things I used to not think twice about… they don’t seem normal to me anymore. They feel marked by it, poisoned by it somehow.\n\nI know that sounds far-fetched. I do understand that if someone else said it, maybe I’d have questions too. But I’m certain of it. It’s like I can pick up an ordinary object and know it’s connected to that house, like it’s carrying whatever was wrong there.\n\nAnd with the neighbors, it’s the same kind of thing. Someone standing outside, a curtain moving, people getting quiet when I come around — maybe other people would say that’s just normal neighborhood stuff. But it doesn’t feel random to me. It feels personal. Like they’re watching to see when I mess up again, waiting for some sign. Other people don’t understand why those little things mean anything, but to me they do. They fit together.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that everyday objects or events in your life were sending you secret messages or had a hidden meaning that was specifically directed at you?\n\nPatient: Yes… I mean, I wouldn’t say it’s like a “message” in words, exactly. It’s not like the toaster is talking to me or a sign is spelling something out. But ordinary things do feel like they have meaning that’s directed at me, or connected to me, in a way other people don’t seem to understand.\n\nLike with the things from the house — my clothes, papers, tools, boxes, even little things I used to use without thinking — they’re not just objects to me anymore. I know it sounds far-fetched, and I know people get that look when I say it, but I’m certain the mold and the wiring in that house poisoned everything I own. So when I see those things now, it feels like they’re carrying something from there. It’s not just “this is a shirt” or “this is a box.” It’s like it has this hidden contamination, this connection to what happened in that house, and it’s aimed right into my life because it’s my stuff. I can’t separate it.\n\nAnd events with the neighbors feel that way too. Like if someone comes outside right when I’m doing something, or they get quiet, or they look over, other people would say, “That’s nothing, that’s just neighbors.” But to me it feels like it means something. Like they’re watching to see when I mess up again. I can tell. It feels directed at me, even if nobody else admits it. So… yes, not secret messages like codes, but hidden meaning, definitely.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that your daily routines, decisions, or actions were subtly influenced or guided by something outside of yourself, like a force or presence that others don’t seem to notice?\n\nPatient: Yes… subtly, yes. Not like there’s some ghost standing there telling me what to do, or like I hear instructions. I don’t mean it that way. But my routines and choices are definitely shaped by it, because once you know something is contaminated, or you know people are watching, you can’t just go about your day like nothing is happening.\n\nLike with the things from that house — and I know, I know how it sounds when I say it out loud — but I’m certain the mold and the wiring in that house poisoned everything I own. So even ordinary decisions get complicated. What clothes I can wear, what papers I touch, what boxes I move, whether I bring something into another room… I have to think about all of it. Other people would just grab a shirt or open a box, but I can’t do that the same way because it’s all carrying something from there.\n\nAnd with the neighbors, that affects me too. I can tell they’re watching to see when I mess up again. So I’ll change what I do, or when I do it, because I don’t want to give them anything. I might wait before going outside, or be extra careful about how I move around, because it feels like if I make one wrong choice they’ll notice. So yes, something outside of me guides my day, but not in a voice or a force exactly. It’s the house, the contamination, and the watching. It’s just… always there.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like your thoughts are being taken out of your head, put into your head, or broadcast out loud for others to hear, even when you aren't speaking them out loud?\n\nPatient: No… not exactly like that. I don’t feel like someone is reaching into my head and pulling thoughts out, or putting brand-new thoughts in there. And I don’t think my thoughts are being broadcast like a radio where everybody can hear the exact words.\n\nWhat I do feel is… people can tell things about me. Especially the neighbors. I can tell they’re watching to see when I mess up again, like they already know something is wrong with me or with my situation. So it’s not that they hear my thoughts word-for-word, but it feels like they’re tuned into me somehow, watching for it, waiting for proof.\n\nAnd with the house, it’s more… I know it sounds far-fetched, but I’m certain the mold and the wiring poisoned everything I own. That’s not a thought somebody put in my head. That’s just something I know from being around it, from what happened to my things. Other people act like I’m imagining it, but I’m not. I know what it did to my clothes, my papers, my boxes, all of it.\n\nSo, no, not thoughts being stolen or spoken out loud. But I do feel like what’s happening to me is obvious to others, and like they’re watching me because of it.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Give candidates as ICD-10 codes, not disease names.\n- If the list changed from the previous candidates, say in \"note\" what this turn added or ruled out.
